(impact) paired TWAP buy over 300 s by market orders (separate streams), 30 pairs x 3 seeds, mid difference in ticks
  300 lots: +15.02 +- 0.33; 1,200 lots: +59.39 +- 0.31; exponent log(I1200 / I300) / log 4 = 0.99
  1,200 lots, 60 s after the end: +59.67 +- 0.35; 300 s after: +59.33 +- 0.50 (remaining share 1.00)
  round trip: buy N lots in one market order, sell N 10 s later, net ticks/lot after fees (raw; paired = minus the twin's mid change)
    N  1: raw -1.118 +- 0.148, paired -1.184 +- 0.053 (90 round trips)
    N 10: raw -0.671 +- 0.144, paired -0.738 +- 0.071 (90 round trips)
    N 30: raw +0.221 +- 0.147, paired +0.154 +- 0.069 (90 round trips)  PROFITS FROM OWN IMPACT
    N 60: raw +1.501 +- 0.172, paired +1.434 +- 0.076 (90 round trips)  PROFITS FROM OWN IMPACT
  note: own-flow value_impact is linear in own lots (value_impact 0.050 x 1,200 lots = +60 ticks of fair value) and own
  market orders fill at pre-impact prices: a round trip of N lots gains ~0.050 N ticks/lot from its own impact; ScalpEnv
  refuses contracts > 1 on presets with value_impact > 0
